## 15. Long-term plan (user, 2026-09-23) — not started

Filed from the user's own plan (provenance **ORIGINAL**). Nothing here is implemented. Items restate or reshape earlier PLAN entries; where they differ, these user statements win. Build one bounded task at a time.

- [ ] **F.01 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — Multiple game modes and multiple maps rather than one map every time. **Setting (user, 2026-09-24): stays fictional; environment variety is wanted, but maps must not be tied to real conflicts.** Relates to M7, M12 (maps built one at a time to the M5 benchmark).
- [ ] **F.02 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — Story mode that progresses and carries meaning across missions, with mission difficulty scaling to the player's position in the story. **Depends on SAVE-01/M9** (nothing persists today) and on F.07 bot difficulty; relates to M10, M12.
- [ ] **F.03 · TODO · ORIGINAL · phase 1 DONE (code, Build 09)** — **Ambush mode**: wave-based survival in the style of CoD Zombies; progressively better weapons, healing and equipment earned during a run; **no win condition — the goal is survival duration**. Scope it to **reuse existing Kohar Valley geometry**, not new maps (this supersedes the rooftop-map assumption in M8; reconcile M8 when scheduled). Run-to-run progression depends on SAVE-01/M9; in-run upgrades do not. Relates to M7.10 (continuous spawning/repositioning) and M3 equipment.
  - Build 09 phase 1 (`dist/ambush.js`, `game.js` Ambush block):
    - **Arena:** solo, in the west district. Four areas: the courtyard with house B1 (start), the field office yard, the west lane with house B9, and the south houses. Three sandbag barricades with timber purchase sections cost 750, 1000 and 1250 points.
    - **Crates:** one per area, selling the four class rifles with unchanged stats for 500, 750, 1000 and 1250 points; a refill costs half. Armor, speed and dressings stay with your class.
    - **Points:** start at 500; a kill is worth 100 and a headshot kill 150. Squadmate kills earn nothing (KILL-01).
    - **Waves:** 4 + 2n enemies (capped at 40), 4 alive at once rising to 7, arrivals every 4.5 s falling to 1.2 s. Aggression rises: they move in sooner, stay down more briefly and take cover closer. They spawn unseen, 35–85 m away and at least 6 m outside the arena, and walk in. There is a 12 s break between waves.
    - **Ending the run:** dying ends it with nothing banked. Five seconds outside the striped line, with a visible countdown, also ends it.
    - **Extract or stay:** from wave 5, a 10 s choice. X extracts and banks points earned × (1 + 0.25 × (waves − 5)); V, or letting the timer run out, stays.
    - **Squad:** squadmates regroup inside the arena after 15 s.
    - **Frame cost:** B6. Tests: T19.
  - Build 10 playtest fixes (`dist/ambush.js` attack tunables, `game.js` `ambushPlan`, `ambushBearing`, `setArenaWalls`):
    - **Attack:** each enemy moves up to its own spot 7 m from you (`holdRange`). Seeing you inside the wave's fight range (26 m at wave 1, narrowing to 14 m) it stops and fires for 2.5–4 s, then moves up again for 3–5 s before it may stop again; while advancing it may duck for .6–1.2 s into cover that gains ground, then rise and fire 1–2 s. Stops shorten with the wave's aggression (`pauseScale`). Within 7 m it stands and fights. Fire cadence, hit chance and damage are the unchanged fire block.
    - **Failsafes:** no 3 m of progress for 8 s → push straight in until within 6 m (`pushAfter`, `pushStop`); no 1 m of movement for 3 s while on the move → step to the nearest navigable spot and re-route (AMB-04); no progress for 30 s while unseen → withdrawn and sent again.
    - **Direction:** every wave picks one of eight bearings from where you stand (enough spawn spots, not last wave's); spawns stay within 40° of it, widened 30° after every 6 failed attempts; the radio says "Wave n inbound from the south-west" and the HUD shows "from SW".
    - **Walls:** the four brick walls in and around the arena are 1.2 m in Ambush (kept out of the static batch; back to 1.7 m and the original geometry in Story/Skirmish). Standing (eye 1.7 m) you see and fire over them; crouched (.98 m) you are hidden and your shot stops at the wall. Enemies standing (1.42 m) see a standing player; a crouched player (.75 m) is hidden from them. They are low cover for enemies only in Ambush.
    - **Not changed:** points, prices, areas, edge line, waves' counts and caps, extract choice.
    - Tests: T20 (8 checks, 22/22 breakages caught). Frame cost: B7. Human feel: **playtested 2026-09-25, wave 7, no stalls, mode confirmed working.**
  - Build 11 difficulty and economy (`dist/ambush.js` `aliveCeiling`, `enemyPool`, `rifleMagazines`, `magBase`/`magWaveStep`/`magWaveCap`, `spawnMaxRoute`, `coverOnRoute`; `game.js` `ambushPool`, `ambushBuyAmmo`, prompts; `dist/records.js`):
    - **Alive cap:** 3 + ⌈n/2⌉ capped at 12: 4 at waves 1–2, one more every two waves, 12 from wave 17 (was 7 from wave 7). Arrival gap floor .8 s from wave 13 (was 1.2). Count per wave, accuracy and damage unchanged. The Ambush enemy pool is 20 actors (the 7 Story soldiers plus 13 attached only while Ambush is played).
    - **Ammunition:** B at any crate buys one magazine for the rifle you hold, into the reserve up to the rifle's limit. Price = 6 points per 100 hp the magazine can deal, × (1 + 0.1 per wave after the first), capped at ×2.5 (wave 16+): carbine 70 → 180, CQB 70 → 160, DMR 100 → 240, automatic rifle 160 → 400. A rifle bought at a crate comes with one magazine loaded and two in reserve (500–1250 points), so re-buying a rifle is never cheaper than magazines. The old half-price full refill is gone; the class rifle still starts full.
    - **Crate prompt:** "E · BUY MK4 DMR · 1000 PTS · 3 MAGAZINES", then the rifle's numbers with the difference from the one held ("78 DMG (+40) · 200 RPM (−371) · 20-RD MAG (−10) · SEMI · 3.0 S RELOAD (+0.5)"), then the magazine line ("B · MAGAZINE FOR MK4 CARBINE · 30 RDS · 70 PTS · RESERVE 120/180"). All read live from `CLASSES`.
    - **Personal best:** the summary ends with "Personal best: wave 9 · 3000 banked · NEW BEST WAVE" (or "First run recorded: …"); stored only when improved; missing, blocked or corrupt storage just leaves the line out.
    - Tests: T21 (6 checks). Frame cost: B8. Human feel: **playtested 2026-09-25:** the squad did the fighting, ammunition ran low by wave 3, the stat line was unreadable, the bandage key undiscoverable → Build 12.
  - Build 12 solo and purchase clarity (`game.js` `ambushSquad`, `KEYS`, `ambushBuyDressing`, prompt; `ambush.js` curve, `dressingPrice`, `rifleMagazines`, `weaponTradeoff`; `style.css` keycaps, dressing line shown in play):
    - **Solo:** no AI squad in Ambush, ever (AMB-07). Squadmates are detached while Ambush is played and restored for the other modes at their original indices.
    - **Rebalance:** alive cap 1 + ⌈n/2⌉ capped at 9 (2 at waves 1–2, 9 from wave 15; was 3 → 12 from wave 17 with the squad), arrival floor 1 s from wave 12 (was .8), enemy pool 16; wave sizes, accuracy and damage unchanged. Probe E24.
    - **Rifles:** five magazines with a rifle bought at a crate, within its reserve (automatic rifle four); no re-buy exploit.
    - **Dressings:** N at any crate, 150 points at wave 1 rising like magazines to 380 from wave 16, kit of five, used with H (+50 hp).
    - **Prompt:** "E · BUY MK4 DMR · 1000 PTS · 5 MAGAZINES" / "MORE DAMAGE · SLOWER FIRE" / blank / "YOUR MK4 CARBINE · RESERVE 120/180 · DRESSINGS 1/5" / "B · MAGAZINE (30 RDS) · 70 PTS   N · FIELD DRESSING · 150 PTS". Trade-off words come from the live weapon data.
    - **Keys:** one `KEYS` map drives bindings and every hint; the vitals panel shows `H` on the dressing count in play (it was hidden unless the map was open), the squad panel shows `Q` as a keycap, reload / extract / stay / crate hints read the map.
    - Tests: T22 (6 checks, 24/24 breakages caught). Frame cost: B9. Human feel: **playtested 2026-09-27: wave 8, 99 kills, 11m10s solo; curve confirmed good.**
  - Build 13 navigation (`ambush.js` `ambushState`, `waypoints`, `buildBeacon`; `game.js` `ambushBeacons`, `ambushMap`, `ambushAreaList`):
    - **Masts:** a 12 m signal mast with crossed unlit panels stands at every barricade that can be bought now (amber with a dark band) and at every crate in an open area (green). The set is re-made from the live state at every purchase and reset; nothing outside Ambush.
    - **Waypoints:** two HUD lines under the objective: "BARRICADE ↗ 34 M · WEST LANE · 1000 PTS" (nearest barricade that can be bought now; "ALL AREAS OPEN" at the end) and "CRATE ← 12 M · MK4 CQB" (nearest crate in an open area). The arrow is relative to where you face.
    - **Map (M):** standing barricades as lines with their price (amber: can be bought; grey: its area is not open yet), closed areas shaded and named, crates as squares; plus an area list in place of the squad panel.
    - **Unchanged:** prices, points, the difficulty curve, solo.
    - Tests: T23. Frame cost: B10. Economy: E25 (report only). Human feel: **playtested 2026-09-27: four signposts at once are clutter, the small map is unreadable, the masts look wrong** → Build 14 removed the masts, the waypoint lines, the area list and the Ambush minimap.
  - Build 14 map and signposting rework (`ambush.js` `MAP`, `mapLayout`, `mapLegend`, `textWidth`, `buildMarking`; `game.js` `ambushMapLayout`, `ambushDrawMap`, `ambushToggleMap`, `ambushMarkings`, `paintedLabel`):
    - **Full-screen map (M):** the whole arena north-up on a 1,000 × 1,600 canvas shown at 86 % of the screen height; open areas green-grey, closed ones dark, each named with OPEN or CLOSED; standing barricades as thick lines (amber: can be bought now; grey: not reachable yet) with their price; crates as squares with the rifle and its price; the dashed amber arena edge; the houses; a white arrow for you. A short legend beside it. The game keeps running while the map is open.
    - **Labels:** placed by a greedy layout that tries positions around each marker and takes the first that overlaps no label, marker or barricade line and lies inside the canvas; text is drawn with its box width as the limit.
    - **In the world:** barricade: amber paint on the top rail of the timber section, a painted board with the price, an oil lantern on the middle post. Crate: a green paint band, a board with the rifle's name, a lantern on a post beside it. Lanterns are unlit glass until the thing can be bought (no light sources: the glass uses an unlit material). Why this choice: the timber section and the crate are where you buy, so the marking is on the object itself; paint and boards are what a village would use; colour matches the map (amber barricade, green crate).
    - **Removed:** signal masts, HUD waypoint lines, HUD area list, the minimap in Ambush.
    - **Still guiding a new player:** "M · Map" in the session controls, the opening radio line, and one radio line per barricade the first time it can be afforded.
    - Tests: T24 (5 checks, 33/33 breakages caught). Frame cost: B11. Human feel UNVERIFIED.
- [ ] **F.04 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — **Custom private lobbies**: invite friends, choose map and match settings. Achievable as an extension of the existing peer-to-peer co-op (NET-03/M11.09); **sequence before any 5v5 work**. Same-build requirement and the TURN limitation (NET-01) apply.
- [ ] **F.05 · BLOCKED · ORIGINAL** — **5v5 team modes** (deathmatch, zone control) with casual queues. **Blocker:** competitive 5v5 needs authoritative dedicated servers, player accounts (AUTH-01), matchmaking and anti-cheat; the current WebRTC peer-to-peer co-op (one host simulating, one guest) cannot support ten players or fair authority. Unblocks only after a server/backend decision.
- [ ] **F.06 · DEFERRED · ORIGINAL** — **Ranked** queue for 5v5. Deferred pending F.05 **and** a player population large enough to rank; do not build before both exist.
- [ ] **F.07 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — **Configurable bot difficulty**, one system shared by story difficulty scaling (F.02) and Ambush wave scaling (F.03). Relates to M7.06.
- [ ] **F.08 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — **Practice / firing range**. Supersedes the SUGGESTION M3.09 with a direct user request.
- [ ] **F.09 · TODO · ORIGINAL · not started** — **Objective variety** beyond the current relay-then-extract pattern. Relates to M12.02 and M10.
- [x] **F.10 · DONE (Build 12) · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-24 / 2026-09-25)** — **Ambush without the AI squad.** Not an option: Ambush is permanently solo for AI purposes (AMB-07). User reasoning (Build 11 playtest): the squad did the killing, so the difficulty never applied to the player. Real players only in any future Ambush co-op (F.11).
- [x] **F.11 · DONE (code, Build 16; two-player playtest pending) · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-24)** — **Ambush co-op** for two real players. Decided by the user: points per player, shared barricades, individual crate purchases; one player down does not end the run. Built as decided, plus: extracting takes both choices; a downed player banks nothing; co-op waves are half as large again while both are up. Original filing: extend the existing two-player peer-to-peer co-op to Ambush (the AMBUSH tab is disabled while a session is connected today). **Real players only: no AI squad joins (AMB-07).** Open design questions, recorded and not decided: **are points shared or per player** (one bank and shared purchases, or individual banks)? **does one player dying end the run**, or does the run continue with a regroup/revive rule like the AI squad's 15 s? Also: the host owns the director, purchases and the extract choice; snapshots carry gates, points and phase; the out-of-bounds rule per player; both players on the same build (change the menu label whenever messages change); NET-01 TURN limits apply.
- [ ] **F.13 · TODO · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-27) · not started** — **Co-op means real players only, in every mode including Story.** No AI squadmates in any co-op session. User reasoning: as in Ambush (AMB-07), bots doing the fighting take the game away from the players. **Consequence to design for:** Story missions are built for four (the player and three squadmates), so a session with fewer real players will be harder; a difficulty option may be needed (relates to F.07 configurable bot difficulty and M7.06). Touches the co-op start (squad detach like `ambushSquad`), snapshots (10 local actors today; the message shape changes, so the menu build label must change), the squad HUD and orders (Q), relay capture and extraction rules written around AI allies. Solo Story and Skirmish keep their squad unless decided otherwise.
- [ ] **F.14 · BLOCKED · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-27) · not started** — **Friends-only PvP in private lobbies, including deathmatch.** Extends private lobbies (F.04) rather than the public 5v5 of F.05. **Blocker: AI-03 must be fixed first** — the co-op guest is hit more often than the host today, and hits are decided by the host; with host-decided hits PvP would be unfair by default (host advantage in hit decisions and latency). Needs: a hit model both players can trust (symmetric hit chance and damage rules, lag-compensated or mutually verified shots), spawn and score rules, and the short-code/lobby service of F.12 for more than two players. User reasoning: play against friends, not strangers; no matchmaking, ranking or anti-cheat service.
- [x] **F.16 · DONE (code, Build 20; two-player playtest pending) · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-28)** — Built in Ambush, Story and Skirmish co-op (REV-01). Original filing: **Reviving a downed teammate in co-op Ambush:** a few seconds standing over them, exposed, brings them back. To decide when it is built: how many seconds, whether firing or being hit interrupts it, how much health the revived player has, whether there is a limit per run or per wave, whether a revived player keeps rifle and points (they do today while down), and what the waves do when the squad is two again (they shrink to the solo size when one goes down).
- [ ] **F.15 · NO LONGER BLOCKED BY PVP-01 (fixed in Build 18) · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-28) · not started** — **Still to settle before a 1v1 is fair:** the host sees its own health and hits at once and the guest a snapshot later; a modified page can use the bounds in E30 (d); the 300 ms cap means that on a slow connection the guest is at a disadvantage and the host is shot "around corners" by up to 300 ms; none of it has been played on a real connection. Earlier text:  **1v1 deathmatch in a private lobby**, offered as an alternative to co-op for the same two connected players, never replacing it. **Blockers: AI-03 (fixed in Build 16 for enemy fire) and PVP-01, the host deciding player-against-player shots, which must be fixed first: with host-decided hits a 1v1 is unfair by default.** Also needs spawn and score rules, a round end, and what the enemies do (none, presumably). Related: F.14 (friends-only PvP), F.04 (private lobbies).
- [ ] **F.12 · TODO · ORIGINAL (user, 2026-09-24) · not started · F.11 is done; Step A reported in E27** — **Short join codes.** The current co-op code is impractically long because the whole WebRTC connection description (offer/answer SDP with ICE candidates) is packed into it. Shortening it requires a small server that holds the description and issues a short key (a signalling/rendezvous service; the game has no backend today). That is the same infrastructure as proper lobbies (F.04) and sessions with more than two players, so it is built once for all three, after Ambush co-op (F.11).

Dependencies and constraints (keep with the items): **SAVE-01 (save/profile system) is a prerequisite for story progression, unlocks and Ambush progression — nothing persists today.** F.05 is BLOCKED for the reason above, not a normal TODO. F.06 is DEFERRED pending player population. F.04 comes before F.05. F.03 reuses Kohar Valley.

**Build 20 (live since 2026-09-29):** this is the build that answers your first two-player session.

Two-player checklist for Build 20 (both reload until the menu says BUILD 20):

1. **Connect once.** Play Story to the end (win or lose), press L: you should both be in the menu, and the line under the mode tabs should say you are connected. Deploy again without codes.
2. **Change the mission** on the same connection: Skirmish, then Ambush, then Story.
3. **Leave in the middle** once as host and once as guest. Nobody should need a new code afterwards.
4. **Go down on purpose.** The other walks over, holds E for four seconds. Say whether four seconds and thirty are right, and whether the downed player can tell what is happening.
5. **Let one clock run out** in Ambush and in Story.
6. **Both go down at once.**
7. **Precision rifle on each other, on purpose:** chest, then shoulder, then head. Chest and shoulder should cost about 70, the head 99 on a Marksman.
8. **Enemy damage:** count the hits it takes to bring one of you down from full health. It should be 7 to 9.
9. **Smoke:** stand in it with F3 open.

**Earlier: play Build 19 solo in Safari (checklist below) and say whether to push it** (pushed 2026-09-29). Then **co-op Ambush with your friend** (Build 18 is live and still unplayed by two people), then **reviving a downed teammate (F.16)**.

Build 19 solo playtest checklist (Safari, `http://localhost:8765/`, the menu says BUILD 19):

1. **Story, first minute:** the line under the ammunition counter shows the sidearm, the knife and your throwables. Say whether it is readable.
2. **Z:** empty the rifle into a wall, press Z instead of R. Say whether the sidearm comes up quickly enough to matter and whether 26 a hit feels too weak or too strong at 10 m and at 30 m.
3. **T:** get behind a patrolling enemy and stab it: one thrust. From the front: two. Say whether 2.3 m feels right.
4. **G, fragmentation:** throw one at a group; then hold G for two seconds and throw. Say whether the arc goes where you expect, and whether the blast is fair through a doorway and behind a wall.
5. **Hold G too long once**, on purpose.
6. **Tab to smoke, G:** throw it between you and enemies who are firing. They should stop. Walk into it: say whether you can see through it, and **watch the frame rate inside the cloud** (F3).
7. **Tab to flash, G:** throw it and look away; then throw one and watch it. Say whether the white screen is too long or too harsh.
8. **Ambush:** you start with the sidearm and no throwables. At a crate: 1, 2 and 3 buy them; with the sidearm in hand B buys its magazine for 30. Say whether the prices compete with magazines and dressings.
9. **Anything that looks wrong in the hand:** the pistol, the knife and the grenade are simple shapes.

**Earlier: say whether to push Build 18** (it contains Build 17; done 2026-09-28), then play co-op Ambush with your friend on BUILD 18. Nothing in co-op has yet been played by two people. After that: **reviving a downed teammate (F.16)**.

Two-player checklist for Build 18, in addition to the Build 16 list below:

1. **Shooting as the guest:** the guest shoots at enemies running across. Aim at them, not ahead of them. Say whether hits land where you aim, and swap who hosts to compare.
2. **Friendly fire on the move:** one strafes, the other fires one aimed shot; then swap. It should feel the same both ways.
3. **Waiting and pausing:** the run should not begin until both of you are in. Either of you pressing P holds it for both, with a notice.
4. **Split up:** one in the courtyard, one in the north houses. Both should keep getting enemies; say if either of you stands idle.
5. **Crouch behind the chest-high wall**, as guest and as host: enemies should lose sight of either of you.
6. **Going down:** watch your friend from behind; walk them along a wall and into a house. The camera should never show the inside of a wall.
7. **Ambush has no AI squad box**; Story and Skirmish still have it.

**Earlier: play Build 16 with your friend (it is live), then say whether Build 17 should be pushed.** Build 16 has not been played by two people. The checklist below is unchanged. Build 17 is committed locally and not pushed; once it is pushed both players must reload to BUILD 17.

Build 17 playtest checklist (solo, Safari, `http://localhost:8765/`, the menu says BUILD 17):

1. **The box:** AI squad sits beside Audio and Blood. Story and Skirmish show it ticked, Ambush unticked.
2. **Story without the squad:** untick it in Story. One amber line warns you. Deploy: nobody spawns beside you. Say how far you get and what kills you.
3. **Ambush with the squad:** tick it in Ambush. Three squadmates start beside you. Watch your points: only your own kills pay. Say by which wave it is too easy or too hard, and whether the squad takes too many kills.
4. **No leak:** switch between the three modes; each remembers its own choice.
5. **Co-op (with your friend, both on BUILD 17):** as host, untick the squad in PRIVATE CO-OP; your friend's box should show your choice, greyed out. Deploy: no squadmates on either screen, your friend is there in blue.
6. **Extraction with one of you down:** from wave 5, one of you goes down on purpose, the other extracts. Both reports should show a bank.

Two-player checklist for Build 16: connect, then the host chooses AMBUSH and DEPLOY BOTH PLAYERS.

Two-player playtest checklist (both on BUILD 16, Safari):

1. **Connect, then AMBUSH.** The guest's Ambush tab follows the host's choice and its button says WAITING FOR HOST.
2. **Same world:** call out the wave number, the direction it comes from and the hostiles left; they should match. Stand next to the same enemy and the same barricade.
3. **Teammate:** blue uniform, blue marker overhead, also through walls. Say whether you could tell friend from enemy in a firefight at 30 m and more, and whether the marker is too large or too small.
4. **Points:** each of you watches only your own points rise for your own kills. The small line under the points shows the teammate's health and points.
5. **Barricade:** one of you clears the first barricade; both walk through. Then the other clears the next one.
6. **Crates:** each buys a rifle, a magazine (B) and a dressing (N); the other's points and rifle must not change.
7. **Friendly fire:** shoot each other once on purpose, host at guest and guest at host, same rifle, body. The health lost should be the same. Say whether it feels fair with movement.
8. **Going down:** one of you dies on purpose. That player should watch the other from behind and be unable to act; the other plays on, against smaller waves. Then the second dies: both get the report, each with their own line.
9. **Line:** one of you stands outside the striped line for five seconds: only that player goes down.
10. **Extract (wave 5 on):** try X by one player only (nothing happens, the other is told), then V by either (you stay), then X by both (you extract, each banks their own points).
11. **Records:** the end report names a co-op best; your solo best must be what it was.
12. **Difficulty:** say whether two players against waves half as large again is too easy, right or too hard, and by which wave.
13. **Interruption:** if the connection stutters, the notice CONNECTION INTERRUPTED · MISSION HELD should appear and play should resume; note how long it lasted.
14. **Story co-op:** the teammate is blue there too, friendly fire is on, and the guest is hit less often than before.

**Earlier: release and retest co-op (COOP-01), done by the user on 2026-09-28.** Build 15 was committed locally and not pushed. When the user says so: `git push`, wait for the run under Actions, then both players reload until the menu says BUILD 15 and try again: host CREATE HOST CODE, COPY CODE, send; teammate paste, JOIN WITH CODE, COPY CODE, send back; host paste, ACCEPT ANSWER. If the status then says the networks could not reach each other, the codes were right and the limit is the network (NET-01).

1. **HUD:** no waypoint lines, no area list, no minimap, no masts. Say whether the screen now feels clean.
2. **Map (M):** it should fill most of the screen. Check that you can read every label, that open and closed areas are told apart at a glance, that each crate names its rifle, and that the white arrow is where you are and points where you look. The game keeps running while it is open: say whether it should pause instead.
